# MarineDebris-Sonar-AI — Prediction-Level Trace & Truncation Diff

**Model:** `sonar_yolov8_fls_trained.pt` (SHA256: `e1215c7e...`)  
**Test Set:** `datasets/sonar_debris_v2_clean` (37 images, 54 ground-truth annotations)

## 1. Summary of Prediction Counts

- **Total Detections at Canonical Benchmark (`conf=0.001`):** 3543
- **Total Detections at Operational Point (`conf=0.25`):** 53
- **Detections Truncated by `conf=0.25` Filter:** 3490

## 2. Truncation Impact on Recall & mAP50

In Ultralytics YOLO, `model.val()` constructs the Precision-Recall curve by ranking all predictions by confidence. When `conf=0.001` is used, the curve traverses 54 ground-truth matches reaching **90.15% Recall** (mAP50 = **89.21%**). When `conf=0.25` is passed, all detections below 0.25 are discarded *prior* to PR curve calculation, capping maximum Recall at **79.33%** and dropping mAP50 to **77.72%**.
